In progressWorldSoccerShop

Shop by Club rail

A rail of club crests with a pop-up that lists every club.

Live demo

Switch the screen width. Photos show only once they are on the image host.

Open in a new tab

Before designers use it

This block needs a review before it goes on a page.

It was copied into the library on 25 Oct 2026 from the canon folder, as it was. Fable and Sacha still need to check it against the paste rules, the five screen widths and contrast. Until then, study it and build from it, but check with Sacha before using it.

The code

This is the build file from the canon folder. It is not a Fastr paste file yet, so it carries its own page around the block.

block.html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
<meta charset="utf-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
<title>Shop by Club Rail, WorldSoccerShop</title>
<!--
  C-29 · Shop by Club rail · WSS · v1 · Shop all: Link
  20 club crests on one swipe rail, Shop All Clubs below. Crests are inlined, no bucket or Fastr calls.
  Shop all option (constant SHOP_ALL in the script): Link goes to /shop/clubs; Pop-up opens the All Clubs lightbox.
  Pop-up works only when placed directly on a new Fastr CMS page; inside an iframe it falls back to Link.
  Heights (content height of the section, for a fixed-height container):
    <640px   216px
    >=640px  251px
    >=1024px 281px
  ⚠️DEVIATION 1: Real Betis links to search (srch?query=real%20betis), no Real Betis page in the link book.
-->

<script>
(function () {
  var root = document.getElementById('scr-wss-v1');
  if (!root || root.hasAttribute('data-scr-ready')) return;
  root.setAttribute('data-scr-ready', '');
  var rail = root.querySelector('[data-scr-rail]');
  var arrows = [].slice.call(root.querySelectorAll('[data-scr-dir]'));
  function sync() {
    var max = rail.scrollWidth - rail.clientWidth;
    root.classList.toggle('has-overflow', max > 2);
    arrows[0].disabled = rail.scrollLeft <= 2;
    arrows[1].disabled = rail.scrollLeft >= max - 2;
  }
  arrows.forEach(function (a) {
    a.addEventListener('click', function () {
      rail.scrollBy({ left: Number(a.getAttribute('data-scr-dir')) * rail.clientWidth * 0.8 });
    });
  });
  rail.addEventListener('scroll', sync, { passive: true });
  window.addEventListener('resize', sync);
  root.classList.add('is-live');
  sync();

  var SHOP_ALL = 'link';
  var framed = false;
  try { framed = window.self !== window.top; } catch (e) { framed = true; }
  var btn = root.querySelector('[data-scr-open]');
  var dlg = document.getElementById('scr-wss-v1-dlg');
  if (SHOP_ALL !== 'popup' || framed || !btn || !dlg || typeof dlg.showModal !== 'function') return;
  var chips = [].slice.call(dlg.querySelectorAll('[role="tab"]'));
  function pick(tab, focus) {
    chips.forEach(function (t) {
      var on = t === tab;
      t.setAttribute('aria-selected', on ? 'true' : 'false');
      if (on) t.removeAttribute('tabindex'); else t.setAttribute('tabindex', '-1');
      document.getElementById(t.getAttribute('aria-controls')).hidden = !on;
    });
    if (focus) tab.focus();
  }
  chips.forEach(function (t, i) {
    t.addEventListener('click', function () { pick(t, false); });
    t.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
      var n = null;
      if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') n = chips[(i + 1) % chips.length];
      else if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') n = chips[(i - 1 + chips.length) % chips.length];
      else if (e.key === 'Home') n = chips[0];
      else if (e.key === 'End') n = chips[chips.length - 1];
      if (n) { e.preventDefault(); pick(n, true); }
    });
  });
  dlg.querySelector('[data-scr-close]').addEventListener('click', function () { dlg.close(); });
  dlg.addEventListener('click', function (e) { if (e.target === dlg) dlg.close(); });
  dlg.addEventListener('close', function () { document.documentElement.style.overflow = ''; });
  btn.setAttribute('aria-haspopup', 'dialog');
  btn.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    dlg.showModal();
    document.documentElement.style.overflow = 'hidden';
  });
})();
</script>
<!-- WSS-CLICK-BEACON — attribution-safe click tracking (rules D2). Posts clicks to the
     parent page; pair with Rules and Resources/fastr-parent-click-listener.html. -->
<script>
(function () {
  var BLOCK = "fastr_blocks_shop_by_club_rail_wss_v1";
  document.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
    var a = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest("a[href]") : null;
    if (!a) return;
    try {
      window.parent.postMessage({
        type: "wss-block-click",
        block: BLOCK,
        label: (a.getAttribute("aria-label") || a.textContent || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim().slice(0, 80),
        href: a.href
      }, "*");
    } catch (err) { /* fire-and-forget */ }
  }, true);
})();
</script>
